Set<SeedRun>();\n\n    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder model)\n    {\n        // Snake_case table and column names are not a style choice: the provided seed.sql\n        // targets these exact names, and executing it verbatim is what lets us treat the\n        // dataset as production data instead of rewriting it.\n        model.Entity<Account>(e =>\n        {\n            e.ToTable(\"accounts\");\n            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);\n            e.Property(x => x.Id).HasColumnName(\"id\").ValueGeneratedNever();\n            e.Property(x => x.Name).HasColumnName(\"name\").HasMaxLength(120).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.Industry).HasColumnName(\"industry\").HasMaxLength(60).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.Timezone).HasColumnName(\"timezone\").HasMaxLength(60).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.CreatedAt).HasColumnName(\"created_at\").HasColumnType(\"datetime2(0)\");\n        });\n\n        model.Entity<ActivityEvent>(e =>\n        {\n            e.ToTable(\"activity_events\");\n            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);\n            e.Property(x => x.Id).HasColumnName(\"id\").ValueGeneratedNever();\n            e.Property(x => x.AccountId).HasColumnName(\"account_id\");\n            e.Property(x => x.Location).HasColumnName(\"location\").HasMaxLength(80).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.EventType).HasColumnName(\"event_type\").HasMaxLength(40).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.OccurredAt).HasColumnName(\"occurred_at\").HasColumnType(\"datetime2(0)\");\n            e.Property(x => x.DurationSeconds).HasColumnName(\"duration_seconds\");\n            e.Property(x => x.Outcome).HasColumnName(\"outcome\").HasMaxLength(40);\n            e.Property(x => x.OccurredLocalDate).HasColumnName(\"occurred_local_date\").HasColumnType(\"date\");\n            e.Property(x => x.LocalWeekStart).HasColumnName(\"local_week_start\").HasColumnType(\"date\");\n\n            e.HasOne(x => x.Account)\n             .WithMany(a => a.Events)\n             .HasForeignKey(x => x.AccountId)\n             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);\n\n            // The pulse query filters on account + local week and groups by location.\n            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.AccountId, x.LocalWeekStart })\n             .HasDatabaseName(\"IX_activity_events_account_local_week\")\n             .IncludeProperties(x => new { x.Location, x.EventType });\n\n            // Deduplication groups on the whole value tuple within an account.\n            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.AccountId, x.Location, x.EventType, x.OccurredAt })\n             .HasDatabaseName(\"IX_activity_events_dedup\");\n        });\n\n        model.Entity<SeedRun>(e =>\n        {\n            e.ToTable(\"seed_runs\");\n            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);\n            e.Property(x => x.Id).HasColumnName(\"id\").ValueGeneratedOnAdd();\n            e.Property(x => x.SourceFile).HasColumnName(\"source_file\").HasMaxLength(260).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.Sha256).HasColumnName(\"sha256\").HasMaxLength(64).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.AccountRows).HasColumnName(\"account_rows\");\n            e.Property(x => x.EventRows).HasColumnName(\"event_rows\");\n            e.Property(x => x.AppliedAtUtc).HasColumnName(\"applied_at_utc\").HasColumnType(\"datetime2(0)\");\n        });\n    }\n}\n"
```json
}
```

```json
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Data\\Seeding\\TimeZoneSegmenter.cs",
  "content": "namespace Relay.Api.Data.Seeding;\n\n/// <summary>A span of UTC time during which an account's zone has one constant offset.</summary>\npublic readonly record struct UtcOffsetSegment(DateTime FromUtc, DateTime ToUtcExclusive, int OffsetMinutes);\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Splits a UTC range into the periods where a given IANA zone holds a constant offset.\n/// This is the whole of the timezone reasoning, deliberately kept as pure C# with no\n/// database involved so it is unit-testable — the seed range spans the 2026-03-08 DST\n/// transition, so a single fixed offset per account would be wrong.\n/// </summary>\npublic static class TimeZoneSegmenter\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Scans hourly. DST transitions in every zone used by the dataset occur on the hour,\n    /// so hourly resolution places each boundary exactly rather than approximately.\n    /// A daily scan would misplace a transition by up to a day and mis-bucket events.\n    /// </summary>\n    public static IReadOnlyList<UtcOffsetSegment> Segment(TimeZoneInfo zone, DateTime fromUtc, DateTime toUtcExclusive)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(zone);\n        if (toUtcExclusive <= fromUtc) return [];\n\n        var start = DateTime.SpecifyKind(fromUtc, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n        var end = DateTime.SpecifyKind(toUtcExclusive, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n\n        var segments = new List<UtcOffsetSegment>();\n        var segmentStart = start;\n        var segmentOffset = OffsetMinutesAt(zone, start);\n\n        for (var cursor = start.AddHours(1); cursor < end; cursor = cursor.AddHours(1))\n        {\n            var offset = OffsetMinutesAt(zone, cursor);\n            if (offset == segmentOffset) continue;\n\n            segments.Add(new UtcOffsetSegment(segmentStart, cursor, segmentOffset));\n            segmentStart = cursor;\n            segmentOffset = offset;\n        }\n\n        segments.Add(new UtcOffsetSegment(segmentStart, end, segmentOffset));\n        return segments;\n    }\n\n    private static int OffsetMinutesAt(TimeZoneInfo zone, DateTime utcInstant)\n        => (int)zone.GetUtcOffset(utcInstant).TotalMinutes;\n}\n"
}
```

```json
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Data\\Seeding\\SeedLoader.cs",
  "content": "using System.Security.Cryptography;\nusing System.Text;\nusing Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Relay.Api.Data.Entities;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Data.Seeding;\n\npublic enum SeedResult { Applied, AlreadyApplied, SkippedDataPresent }\n\npublic sealed record SeedOutcome(SeedResult Result, int AccountRows, int EventRows, string Sha256);\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Loads the provided seed.sql without modifying it.\n///\n/// The file is ~12.6k bare INSERTs carrying explicit ids, so a second run would violate\n/// the primary key. The brief says to treat it as production data, so idempotency comes\n/// from wrapping the load — a hash-stamped marker row in seed_runs — rather than from\n/// rewriting the file with IF NOT EXISTS guards.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class SeedLoader(RelayDbContext db, ILogger<SeedLoader> logger)\n{\n    private const int StatementsPerBatch = 500;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Derives the account-local calendar date and Monday week start for one constant-offset\n    /// span. Set-based: one statement per (account, DST segment), so ~2 per account rather\n    /// than 12.6k row updates. 1900-01-01 was a Monday, which makes the week start\n    /// arithmetic independent of the connection's DATEFIRST setting.\n    /// </summary>\n    private const string FillLocalDatesSql = \"\"\"\n        UPDATE e\n           SET occurred_local_date = d.local_date,\n               local_week_start    = DATEADD(DAY, -(DATEDIFF(DAY, '19000101', d.local_date) % 7), d.local_date)\n          FROM activity_events AS e\n         CROSS APPLY (SELECT CAST(DATEADD(MINUTE, @offsetMinutes, e.occurred_at) AS date)) AS d(local_date)\n         WHERE e.account_id  = @accountId\n           AND e.occurred_at >= @fromUtc\n           AND e.occurred_at <  @toUtc;\n        \"\"\";\n\n    public async Task<SeedOutcome> EnsureSeededAsync(string seedSqlPath, CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        if (!File.Exists(seedSqlPath))\n            throw new FileNotFoundException($\"Seed file not found. Expected it at '{seedSqlPath}'.\", seedSqlPath);\n\n        var sql = await File.ReadAllTextAsync(seedSqlPath, ct);\n        var sha = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(sql))).ToLowerInvariant();\n\n        if (await db.SeedRuns.AnyAsync(r => r.Sha256 == sha, ct))\n        {\n            var existing = await db.SeedRuns.AsNoTracking().FirstAsync(r => r.Sha256 == sha, ct);\n            logger.LogInformation(\"Seed {Sha} already applied at {At}; skipping.\", sha[..12], existing.AppliedAtUtc);\n            return new SeedOutcome(SeedResult.AlreadyApplied, existing.AccountRows, existing.EventRows, sha);\n        }\n\n        // Data from some other source is present. Refuse rather than half-load on top of it.\n        if (await db.Accounts.AnyAsync(ct))\n        {\n            logger.LogWarning(\"Tables already hold data that did not come from seed {Sha}; skipping load.\", sha[..12]);\n            return new SeedOutcome(SeedResult.SkippedDataPresent, 0, 0, sha);\n        }\n\n        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);\n\n        await ExecuteSeedStatementsAsync(sql, ct);\n        await FillLocalDatesAsync(ct);\n        await AssertNoUnbucketedEventsAsync(ct);\n\n        var accountRows = await db.Accounts.CountAsync(ct);\n        var eventRows = await db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync(ct);\n\n        db.SeedRuns.Add(new SeedRun\n        {\n            SourceFile = Path.GetFileName(seedSqlPath),\n            Sha256 = sha,\n            AccountRows = accountRows,\n            EventRows = eventRows,\n            AppliedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow\n        });\n        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);\n\n        logger.LogInformation(\"Seeded {Accounts} accounts and {Events} events from {File}.\",\n            accountRows, eventRows, Path.GetFileName(seedSqlPath));\n\n        return new SeedOutcome(SeedResult.Applied, accountRows, eventRows, sha);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Executes the file in batches. One round trip per statement would be 12.6k\n    /// round trips; the file has no GO separators and every statement is one line.</summary>\n    private async Task ExecuteSeedStatementsAsync(string sql, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var statements = sql\n            .Split('\\n')\n            .Select(line => line.Trim())\n            .Where(line => line.Length > 0 && !line.StartsWith(\"--\", StringComparison.Ordinal))\n            .ToList();\n\n        foreach (var batch in statements.Chunk(StatementsPerBatch))\n            await db.Database.ExecuteSqlRawAsync(string.Join('\\n', batch), ct);\n    }\n\n    private async Task FillLocalDatesAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var accounts = await db.Accounts.AsNoTracking().ToListAsync(ct);\n\n        foreach (var account in accounts)\n        {\n            var bounds = await db.ActivityEvents\n                .Where(e => e.AccountId == account.Id)\n                .GroupBy(_ => 1)\n                .Select(g => new { Min = g.Min(e => e.OccurredAt), Max = g.Max(e => e.OccurredAt) })\n                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);\n\n            // Quiet Harbor Spa (id 20) has no events at all. Not an error — an empty account\n            // is a case the dashboard has to render, so it must survive seeding untouched.\n            if (bounds is null) continue;\n\n            var zone = TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(account.Timezone);\n            var segments = TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(zone, bounds.Min, bounds.Max.AddSeconds(1));\n\n            foreach (var segment in segments)\n            {\n                await db.Database.ExecuteSqlRawAsync(FillLocalDatesSql,\n                [\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@offsetMinutes\", segment.OffsetMinutes),\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@accountId\", account.Id),\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@fromUtc\", segment.FromUtc),\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@toUtc\", segment.ToUtcExclusive)\n                ], ct);\n            }\n\n            logger.LogDebug(\"Account {Id} ({Zone}): {Segments} offset segment(s).\",\n                account.Id, account.Timezone, segments.Count);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>The local-date columns are nullable only because seed.sql cannot supply them.\n    /// Nothing downstream tolerates a null, so a gap is a hard failure at load time rather\n    /// than a silently missing row in an aggregate.</summary>\n    private async Task AssertNoUnbucketedEventsAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var unbucketed = await db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync(e => e.OccurredLocalDate == null, ct);\n        if (unbucketed > 0)\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                $\"{unbucketed} event(s) have no local date after seeding. Every event must fall inside \" +\n                \"one of its account's offset segments; a gap means the segmentation is wrong.\");\n    }\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Analytics\\Sql\\WeeklyTotalsByLocation.sql",
  "content": "-- Weekly event totals per location, for one account, across the current week and the\n-- N baseline weeks immediately before it.\n--\n-- Contract with the C# caller (PLAN.md Amendment 2): SQL aggregates as far as weekly\n-- totals and stops there. Median, MAD, the typical band and the status are computed in\n-- C# over this result set, which is at most (locations x weeks) rows -- 15 x 13 for the\n-- largest account in the seed.\n--\n-- Parameters:\n--   @accountId        int   -- always scoped: 'Site A' exists in 19 different accounts\n--   @currentWeekStart date  -- Monday of the week under review, account-local\n--   @windowStart      date  -- Monday of the oldest baseline week (computed by the caller\n--                              so this file stays a single composable statement)\n--   @baselineWeeks    int   -- 4 | 8 | 12\n--   @eventType        nvarchar(40) -- 'all', or one of the three event types\n--\n-- Three things this has to get right, each measured in analysis/:\n--   1. DEDUPLICATION. 12 exact value-duplicates carry distinct ids. Counting both copies\n--      inflates whichever baseline week they land in.\n--   2. GAP FILLING. GROUP BY returns only weeks that have rows. A location that went\n--      silent for a week must produce 0, not disappear -- a median taken over 5 of 8\n--      weeks is wrong and looks perfectly fine.\n--   3. LOCAL WEEKS. Buckets come from local_week_start, derived at load time in the\n--      account's own timezone, never from occurred_at directly.\n\nWITH week_offsets AS (\n    -- Recursive rather than GENERATE_SERIES: that function needs compatibility level 160,\n    -- and this stays runnable on older SQL Server. Depth is at most 12.\n    SELECT 0 AS n\n    UNION ALL\n    SELECT n + 1 FROM week_offsets WHERE n < @baselineWeeks\n),\nweeks AS (\n    SELECT CAST(DATEADD(WEEK, -n, @currentWeekStart) AS date) AS week_start\n    FROM week_offsets\n),\nlocations AS (\n    -- Any location with activity somewhere in the window. One that has baseline volume but\n    -- went quiet this week still appears -- that is precisely the location needing\n    -- attention. One with no activity anywhere in the window drops off instead of reading\n    -- as \"-100%\" forever.\n    SELECT DISTINCT location\n    FROM activity_events\n    WHERE account_id = @accountId\n      AND local_week_start BETWEEN @windowStart AND @currentWeekStart\n),\nspine AS (\n    SELECT l.location, w.week_start\n    FROM locations AS l\n    CROSS JOIN weeks AS w\n),\ndeduped AS (\n    -- Keep the lowest id of each exact value tuple. GROUP BY treats NULLs as equal, which\n    -- is what we want: duplicates with a NULL outcome or duration must collapse too.\n    SELECT MIN(id) AS id\n    FROM activity_events\n    WHERE account_id = @accountId\n      AND local_week_start BETWEEN @windowStart AND @currentWeekStart\n    GROUP BY location, event_type, occurred_at, duration_seconds, outcome\n),\ntotals AS (\n    SELECT e.location,\n           e.local_week_start AS week_start,\n           COUNT_BIG(*) AS event_count\n    FROM activity_events AS e\n    INNER JOIN deduped AS d ON d.id = e.id\n    WHERE @eventType = 'all' OR e.event_type = @eventType\n    GROUP BY e.location, e.local_week_start\n)\nSELECT s.location                              AS Location,\n       s.week_start                            AS WeekStart,\n       CAST(ISNULL(t.event_count, 0) AS int)   AS EventCount\nFROM spine AS s\nLEFT JOIN totals AS t\n       ON t.location   = s.location\n      AND t.week_start = s.week_start\nORDER BY s.location, s.week_start;\n"
}
```

```json
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Analytics\\BaselineCalculator.cs",
  "content": "namespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\npublic enum PulseStatus\n{\n    /// <summary>Inside the typical band.</summary>\n    Normal,\n    Above,\n    Below,\n\n    /// <summary>Baseline median below the low-volume threshold. Counts and band are still\n    /// shown, but no status badge: a location going 5 -> 9 is \"+64%\" and is also noise.</summary>\n    LowVolume,\n\n    /// <summary>Fewer complete weeks of history than the baseline needs to mean anything.</summary>\n    InsufficientHistory,\n\n    /// <summary>Baseline median is zero. There is no percentage change from zero.</summary>\n    NoBaseline\n}\n\npublic sealed record BaselineOptions\n{\n    public int MinHistoryWeeks { get; init; } = 4;\n    public int LowVolumeThreshold { get; init; } = 5;\n\n    /// <summary>Half-width of the typical band, in robust standard deviations. 2 gives\n    /// roughly a 95% band for normally distributed counts.</summary>\n    public double BandSigmas { get; init; } = 2.0;\n\n    public static BaselineOptions Default { get; } = new();\n}\n\npublic sealed record BaselineAssessment\n{\n    public required int Current { get; init; }\n    public required int BaselineWeeksUsed { get; init; }\n    public required double BaselineMedian { get; init; }\n    public required double Mad { get; init; }\n    public required double Spread { get; init; }\n    public required double TypicalLow { get; init; }\n    public required double TypicalHigh { get; init; }\n\n    /// <summary>Null when there is no meaningful baseline to divide by. Never infinity,\n    /// never NaN, never a fabricated -100%.</summary>\n    public double? DeltaPct { get; init; }\n\n    /// <summary>Signed distance from the baseline in spread units. Used to rank locations\n    /// by how unusual they are rather than by raw volume. Null when not comparable.</summary>\n    public double? DeviationScore { get; init; }\n\n    public required PulseStatus Status { get; init; }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Turns a location's weekly totals into \"is this normal for us?\".\n///\n/// Deliberately pure and database-free (PLAN.md Amendment 2) so every branch is unit\n/// testable. Two of the branches — InsufficientHistory and NoBaseline — have no real data\n/// behind them anywhere in the seed (minimum baseline median across all 69 account/location\n/// pairs is 3.5, minimum history 24 weeks), so their tests use synthetic fixtures. That is\n/// recorded in PLAN.md Amendment 1 rather than papered over.\n/// </summary>\npublic static class BaselineCalculator\n{\n    /// <summary>Scales MAD to a standard-deviation equivalent for a normal distribution.</summary>\n    private const double MadToSigma = 0.6745;\n\n    public static BaselineAssessment Assess(\n        int current,\n        IReadOnlyList<int> baselineWeeklyTotals,\n        BaselineOptions? options = null)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(baselineWeeklyTotals);\n        var opts = options ?? BaselineOptions.Default;\n\n        if (baselineWeeklyTotals.Count < opts.MinHistoryWeeks)\n        {\n            return new BaselineAssessment\n            {\n                Current = current,\n                BaselineWeeksUsed = baselineWeeklyTotals.Count,\n                BaselineMedian = 0,\n                Mad = 0,\n                Spread = 0,\n                TypicalLow = 0,\n                TypicalHigh = 0,\n                DeltaPct = null,\n                DeviationScore = null,\n                Status = PulseStatus.InsufficientHistory\n            };\n        }\n\n        // Median, not mean. Account 6 carries 805 events on 2026-06-03 against a typical\n        // 10.9/day; that single day moves a mean-based weekly baseline by ~3.6x and leaves\n        // the median untouched. Seven of the eight low-volume locations in the seed carry\n        // that burst week inside their default baseline window, so this is not theoretical.\n        var median = Median(baselineWeeklyTotals);\n\n        if (median == 0)\n        {\n            return new BaselineAssessment\n            {\n                Current = current,\n                BaselineWeeksUsed = baselineWeeklyTotals.Count,\n                BaselineMedian = 0,\n                Mad = 0,\n                Spread = 0,\n                TypicalLow = 0,\n                TypicalHigh = 0,\n                DeltaPct = null,          // dividing by zero is not a percentage\n                DeviationScore = null,\n                Status = PulseStatus.NoBaseline\n            };\n        }\n\n        var mad = Median(baselineWeeklyTotals.Select(x => Math.Abs(x - median)).ToList());\n\n        // MAD collapses to zero whenever a MAJORITY of weeks share a value, not only when\n        // all of them do — account 12 / Site A reads [8,8,8,8,3,6,8,14], median 8, MAD 0,\n        // with real spread present. Without a fallback that location would flag 3 and 14 as\n        // extreme. sqrt(median) is the Poisson-ish spread for count data.\n        var spread = mad > 0 ? mad / MadToSigma : Math.Sqrt(median);\n\n        var halfWidth = opts.BandSigmas * spread;\n        var typicalLow = Math.Max(0, median - halfWidth);   // a week cannot hold fewer than 0 events\n        var typicalHigh = median + halfWidth;\n\n        var status = median < opts.LowVolumeThreshold\n            ? PulseStatus.LowVolume\n            : current < typicalLow ? PulseStatus.Below\n            : current > typicalHigh ? PulseStatus.Above\n            : PulseStatus.Normal;\n\n        return new BaselineAssessment\n        {\n            Current = current,\n            BaselineWeeksUsed = baselineWeeklyTotals.Count,\n            BaselineMedian = median,\n            Mad = mad,\n            Spread = spread,\n            TypicalLow = typicalLow,\n            TypicalHigh = typicalHigh,\n            DeltaPct = (current - median) / median,\n            DeviationScore = spread > 0 ? (current - median) / spread : 0,\n            Status = status\n        };\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Even-sized sets take the mean of the two central values.</summary>\n    public static double Median(IReadOnlyList<int> values)\n    {\n        if (values.Count == 0) throw new ArgumentException(\"Median of an empty set is undefined.\", nameof(values));\n\n        var sorted = values.OrderBy(v => v).ToArray();\n        var mid = sorted.Length / 2;\n        return sorted.Length % 2 == 1\n            ? sorted[mid]\n            : (sorted[mid - 1] + sorted[mid]) / 2.0;\n    }\n\n    private static double Median(IReadOnlyList<double> values)\n    {\n        var sorted = values.OrderBy(v => v).ToArray();\n        var mid = sorted.Length / 2;\n        return sorted.Length % 2 == 1\n            ? sorted[mid]\n            : (sorted[mid - 1] + sorted[mid]) / 2.0;\n    }\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Analytics\\WeekMath.cs",
  "content": "namespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\n/// <summary>Monday-start week arithmetic, in whatever calendar the caller already resolved\n/// to (always account-local here — never UTC).</summary>\npublic static class WeekMath\n{\n    public static DateOnly MondayOf(DateOnly date)\n        => date.AddDays(-(((int)date.DayOfWeek + 6) % 7));\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// The most recent Monday–Sunday week that is fully covered by data ending on\n    /// <paramref name=\"lastLocalDate\"/>. The seed ends on Monday 2026-07-27, so this\n    /// returns 2026-07-20 — the week just gone, which is what a customer admin reviews on\n    /// a Monday morning. Anchoring to the system clock instead would render an empty\n    /// dashboard, since the data is months behind wall time.\n    /// </summary>\n    public static DateOnly LastCompleteWeekStart(DateOnly lastLocalDate)\n    {\n        var monday = MondayOf(lastLocalDate);\n        return lastLocalDate >= monday.AddDays(6)\n            ? monday                 // data runs through Sunday: that week is complete\n            : monday.AddDays(-7);    // partial week in progress: fall back one\n    }\n}\n"
}
```

```json
  "content": "using System.Data;\nusing System.Reflection;\nusing Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Relay.Api.Data;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\n/// <summary>One row of the SQL aggregate. A flat DTO, deliberately not an entity, so EF\n/// change tracking never touches it.</summary>\npublic sealed class WeeklyLocationTotal\n{\n    public string Location { get; set; } = string.Empty;\n    public DateOnly WeekStart { get; set; }\n    public int EventCount { get; set; }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Runs the weekly-totals aggregate. The SQL lives in its own embedded .sql file rather\n/// than in a string literal (PLAN.md Amendment 4) so it can be executed straight against\n/// the database to verify its numbers — which matters, because correct aggregates are the\n/// thing being judged.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class WeeklyTotalsQuery(RelayDbContext db)\n{\n    public static readonly string[] AllowedEventTypes =\n        [\"all\", \"call_received\", \"lead_created\", \"appointment_set\"];\n\n    private static readonly string Sql = EmbeddedSql.Load(\"Analytics.Sql.WeeklyTotalsByLocation.sql\");\n\n    /// <summary>Called at startup so a missing or renamed resource fails immediately with a\n    /// useful message rather than on the first request.</summary>\n    public static void EnsureSqlLoaded() => _ = Sql.Length;\n\n    public Task<List<WeeklyLocationTotal>> RunAsync(\n        int accountId,\n        DateOnly currentWeekStart,\n        int baselineWeeks,\n        string eventType,\n        CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        if (!AllowedEventTypes.Contains(eventType))\n            throw new ArgumentException($\"Unsupported event type '{eventType}'.\", nameof(eventType));\n\n        // Computed here rather than DECLAREd in the .sql so that file stays a single\n        // statement — EF composes over raw SQL, and a multi-statement batch would break it.\n        var windowStart = currentWeekStart.AddDays(-7 * baselineWeeks);\n\n        return db.Database.SqlQueryRaw<WeeklyLocationTotal>(Sql,\n            new SqlParameter(\"@accountId\", SqlDbType.Int) { Value = accountId },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@currentWeekStart\", SqlDbType.Date) { Value = currentWeekStart.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@windowStart\", SqlDbType.Date) { Value = windowStart.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@baselineWeeks\", SqlDbType.Int) { Value = baselineWeeks },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@eventType\", SqlDbType.NVarChar, 40) { Value = eventType }\n        ).ToListAsync(ct);\n    }\n}\n\ninternal static class EmbeddedSql\n{\n    public static string Load(string relativeName)\n    {\n        var assembly = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();\n        var resourceName = $\"{assembly.GetName().Name}.{relativeName}\";\n\n        using var stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(resourceName);\n        if (stream is null)\n        {\n            var available = string.Join(Environment.NewLine + \"  \", assembly.GetManifestResourceNames());\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                $\"Embedded SQL resource '{resourceName}' was not found. Resource names are derived from \" +\n                $\"the file path, so a moved or renamed .sql breaks this silently at runtime.{Environment.NewLine}\" +\n                $\"Available resources:{Environment.NewLine}  {available}\");\n        }\n\n        using var reader = new StreamReader(stream);\n        return reader.ReadToEnd();\n    }\n}\n"

DeltaPct,\n    double? DeviationScore,\n    int BaselineWeeksUsed,\n    string Status);\n\npublic sealed record LocationPulse(string Location, MetricView Metric);\n\n/// <summary>Surfaced rather than hidden: the API says out loud how many rows it dropped and\n/// whether the account has any data at all, so a reader can tell an empty account from a\n/// broken query.</summary>\npublic sealed record DataQuality(\n    bool HasData,\n    int DuplicateEventsExcluded,\n    DateOnly?   "content": "using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Relay.Api.Contracts;\nusing Relay.Api.Data;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\npublic sealed class PulseService(RelayDbContext db, WeeklyTotalsQuery totals)\n{\n    public static readonly int[] AllowedBaselineWeeks = [4, 8, 12];\n\n    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AccountListItem>> ListAccountsAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)\n        => await db.Accounts\n            .AsNoTracking()\n            .OrderBy(a => a.Name)\n            .Select(a => new AccountListItem(\n                a.Id,\n                a.Name,\n                a.Industry,\n                a.Timezone,\n                a.Events.Select(e => e.Location).Distinct().Count(),\n                a.Events.Any(),\n                a.Events.Max(e => e.OccurredLocalDate)))\n            .ToListAsync(ct);\n\n    /// <summary>Returns null when the account does not exist. An account that exists but has\n    /// no events is NOT null — it is a real state the dashboard has to render, and Quiet\n    /// Harbor Spa in the seed is exactly that.</summary>\n    public async Task<WeeklyPulseResponse?> GetWeeklyPulseAsync(\n        int accountId,\n        DateOnly? requestedWeekStart,\n        int baselineWeeks,\n        string eventType,\n        CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        var account = await db.Accounts.AsNoTracking()\n            .Where(a => a.Id == accountId)\n            .Select(a => new { a.Id, a.Name, a.Industry, a.Timezone })\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);\n\n        if (account is null) return null;\n\n        var bounds = await db.ActivityEvents\n            .Where(e => e.AccountId == accountId)\n            .GroupBy(_ => 1)\n            .Select(g => new\n            {\n                Earliest = g.Min(e => e.OccurredLocalDate),\n                Latest = g.Max(e => e.OccurredLocalDate),\n                Locations = g.Select(e => e.Location).Distinct().Count()\n            })\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);\n\n        var hasData = bounds is not null && bounds.Latest is not null;\n\n        // \"Now\" comes from the data, never from the clock — the seed ends 2026-07-27 and the\n        // system clock would produce an empty dashboard. When the account itself is empty we\n        // still need a week to render, so fall back to the newest date anywhere in the set.\n        var anchorDate = bounds?.Latest\n                         ?? await db.ActivityEvents.MaxAsync(e => e.OccurredLocalDate, ct);\n\n        var weekStart = requestedWeekStart is { } requested\n            ? WeekMath.MondayOf(requested)\n            : anchorDate is { } anchor\n                ? WeekMath.LastCompleteWeekStart(anchor)\n                : WeekMath.LastCompleteWeekStart(DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.UtcNow));\n\n        var windowStart = weekStart.AddDays(-7 * baselineWeeks);\n\n        var accountItem = new AccountListItem(\n            account.Id, account.Name, account.Industry, account.Timezone,\n            bounds?.Locations ?? 0, hasData, bounds?.Latest);\n\n        var week = new WeekWindow(weekStart, weekStart.AddDays(6));\n        var baselineWindow = new BaselineWindow(baselineWeeks, windowStart, weekStart.AddDays(-1));\n\n        if (!hasData)\n        {\n            return new WeeklyPulseResponse(\n                accountItem, week, baselineWindow, eventType,\n                Total: null,\n                Locations: [],\n                DataQuality: new DataQuality(false, 0, null, null));\n        }\n\n        var rows = await totals.RunAsync(accountId, weekStart, baselineWeeks, eventType, ct);\n        var duplicates = await CountDuplicatesInWindowAsync(accountId, windowStart, weekStart, ct);\n\n        var locations = rows\n            .GroupBy(r => r.Location, StringComparer.Ordinal)\n            .Select(g =>\n            {\n                var current = g.FirstOrDefault(r => r.WeekStart == weekStart)?.EventCount ?? 0;\n                var baseline = g.Where(r => r.WeekStart < weekStart)\n                                .OrderBy(r => r.WeekStart)\n                                .Select(r => r.EventCount)\n                                .ToList();\n                return new LocationPulse(g.Key, ToView(BaselineCalculator.Assess(current, baseline)));\n            })\n            .OrderBy(l => ActionPriority(l.Metric.Status))\n            .ThenByDescending(l => Math.Abs(l.Metric.DeviationScore ?? 0))\n            .ThenBy(l => l.Location, StringComparer.Ordinal)\n            .ToList();\n\n        // The account roll-up is the same comparison applied to the sum across locations.\n        // Summing the gap-filled per-location totals is what makes this correct: a location\n        // missing from a week contributes 0 rather than being absent from the sum.\n        var byWeek = rows.GroupBy(r => r.WeekStart)\n                         .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Sum(r => r.EventCount));\n\n        var accountCurrent = byWeek.GetValueOrDefault(weekStart);\n        var accountBaseline = byWeek.Where(kv => kv.Key < weekStart)\n                                    .OrderBy(kv => kv.Key)\n                                    .Select(kv => kv.Value)\n                                    .ToList();\n\n        return new WeeklyPulseResponse(\n            accountItem, week, baselineWindow, eventType,\n            Total: ToView(BaselineCalculator.Assess(accountCurrent, accountBaseline)),\n            Locations: locations,\n            DataQuality: new DataQuality(true, duplicates, bounds!.Earliest, bounds.Latest));\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Diagnostic only — the pulse aggregate itself runs through the embedded SQL. Reported\n    /// so the deduplication is visible in the payload instead of silently dropping rows.\n    /// </summary>\n    private async Task<int> CountDuplicatesInWindowAsync(\n        int accountId, DateOnly windowStart, DateOnly weekStart, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var inWindow = db.ActivityEvents.Where(e =>\n            e.AccountId == accountId &&\n            e.LocalWeekStart >= windowStart &&\n            e.LocalWeekStart <= weekStart);\n\n        var raw = await inWindow.CountAsync(ct);\n        var distinct = await inWindow\n            .Select(e => new { e.Location, e.EventType, e.OccurredAt, e.DurationSeconds, e.Outcome })\n            .Distinct()\n            .CountAsync(ct);\n\n        return raw - distinct;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Locations that need a decision come first; suppressed ones sink.</summary>\n    private static int ActionPriority(string status) => status switch\n    {\n        \"below\" or \"above\" => 0,\n        \"normal\" => 1,\n        \"lowVolume\" => 2,\n        _ => 3\n    };\n\n    private static MetricView ToView(BaselineAssessment a) => new(\n        a.Current,\n        Math.Round(a.BaselineMedian, 2),\n        Math.Round(a.TypicalLow, 2),\n        Math.Round(a.TypicalHigh, 2),\n        a.DeltaPct is { } d ? Math.Round(d, 4) : null,\n        a.DeviationScore is { } s ? Math.Round(s, 3) : null,\n        a.BaselineWeeksUsed,\n        Camel(a.Status.ToString()));\n\n    private static string Camel(string s) => char.ToLowerInvariant(s[0]) + s[1..];\n}\n"
}
```

## ASSISTANT  <sub>08/14/2026 01:07:20</sub>

El roll-up de cuenta verificado a mano contra la serie cruda:

```
baseline (8 sem) : 53, 881, 102, 59, 76, 69, 79, 50
ordenado         : 50, 53, 59, 69, 76, 79, 102, 881   → mediana = (69+76)/2 = 72.5  ✓
desviaciones     : 3.5, 3.5, 6.5, 13.5, 19.5, 22.5, 29.5, 808.5 → MAD = 16.5  ✓
spread           : 16.5/0.6745 = 24.46 → banda = 72.5 ± 48.93 = [23.57, 121.43]  ✓
actual 87        → dentro de banda → normal, +20%  ✓
```

**Y ahí está la demostración con datos reales:** la semana del burst (881) está dentro del baseline. Una media daría `1369/8 = 171,1`, y esta semana leería **−49%, colapso**. La mediana dice **+20%, normal**. Esa es la pantalla por defecto de la cuenta 6, no un fixture.

Casos límite: cuenta 20 devuelve `hasData=false`, `total=null`, 0 sedes y una semana válida — no un 500 ni un `NaN`. Cuenta 16 (una sola sede, la más dispersa) resuelve limpio.
